| kernel-check-shell-key | KernelEngineer | verified | 无 | 完成10文件范围的最小已知原始key守门。Check/Shell Service经现有public factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit 224pass/0fail/0skip，exit0且candidateUnchanged=true；candidate b606d67d64280fcecbaf32b572d34435288911fe9fb3c4cc87b996fd44a9348f，evidence .ans/runtime/legion-cli-role-owned-v2-key-guard-integration/evidence/kernel-check-shell-key-1-unit-e8354e9d.log SHA5e832be8f3f1c520c251cc7c14421f57bd66606d79e559ed6b739b157aef8dce。API/changelog诚实限定指定已知原始值路径：可变Check客户输入TOCTOU与子进程从编码/片段/允许网络重构后直写tmp/outputs不由四impl保证，不能宣称全称从未落盘。不以事后scan/skip/裸Shell冒充。Desktop下一阶段须在自身两factory传当前完整knownKeys快照；新值需重建Service。旧包、日志与partial保留；未verify/dispatch。 |

Node EnvironmentProfiles public resolve runs before first getState/history restore; Pipeline never reads process.env. Desktop encrypted metadata browsing stays read-only, active connection keys register before Human recording or role execution. Human valid commands including message/answer/profile/root/mapping/context/confirmation reject known values before invalidation, writes or grants; no silent redaction. Tool/control requests, PM/member text, role proposal/review, Acceptance/repair, roleMemories/summary, facts and Supervisor reject before persistence/DTO/effects. Each run buffers text privately until safe terminal. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Explicit stop/switch suppresses new terminal monitor and cancels/drains existing monitors, eliminating observed organization_busy after stop while preserving async success/failure recommendations and ready-set parallelism. Organization/Project/Memory Service/Provider private knownSecrets data guard docs/read/CAS/returned facts; no IO callback/grant expansion. No edits to Kernel/Adapter or package inputs. Real random canaries exercised public Node first state/contaminated restore, 45000-char safe Human, actual Human question-answer, split SDK delta no prefix persisted/clean next turn, interaction/proposal/member/Supervisor, Source write/reconstructed edit, fact/Acceptance, automatic summary old memory unchanged, customer read original bytes, native Check and Shell stdout/stderr reconstruction with private knownKeys. Pure actual terminal merge regression covers non-prefix halves. Final isolated installed CLI/package and independent integration/review remain subsequent owner gates. |

Service经现有public factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit 224pass/0fail/0skip，exit0且candidateUnchanged=true；candidate b606d67d64280fcecbaf32b572d34435288911fe9fb3c4cc87b996fd44a9348f，evidence factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit Service经现有public factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit 224pass/0fail/0skip，exit0且candidateUnchanged=true；candidate b606d67d64280fcecbaf32b572d34435288911fe9fb3c4cc87b996fd44a9348f，evidence factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit Service经现有public factory私传knownKeys，Provider复制为有预算的私有字节匹配，不入Model/授权/digest/env/log/DTO。Shell在原客户输入copy镜像前拒绝当时命中值；父进程原始与净化stdout/stderr逐流+跨管道有序尾缓冲在积累/回调/output.json前阻断，命中停止且不写污染log。Check在现有inputVersions读取核验时命中则不spawn，父输出/已认证event字段与跨chunk/管道先守门再形成结果。四套既有测试新增8项真实随机canary路径，含静态输入copy拒绝、stdout/stderr各自split及两管道mixed真实子进程输出；检查回调/本run私有普通文件无原始值，客户原bytes/hash不变，正常无key及macOS隔离原检查保留。首次224项223pass/1fail的原伪造reporter事件拒绝状态已恢复原语义，断言未放宽。最终固定unit 224pass/0fail/0skip，exit0且candidateUnchanged=true；candidate b606d67d64280fcecbaf32b572d34435288911fe9fb3c4cc87b996fd44a9348f，evidence exact known-process-key guards in the unique Host, with runtime0.9.2 private Check/Shell knownKeys factory wiring. Node EnvironmentProfiles public resolve runs before first getState/history restore; Pipeline never reads process.env. Desktop encrypted metadata browsing stays read-only, active connection keys register before Human recording or role execution. Human valid commands including message/answer/profile/root/mapping/context/confirmation reject known values before invalidation, writes or grants; no silent redaction. Tool/control requests, PM/member text, role proposal/review, Acceptance/repair, roleMemories/summary, facts and Supervisor reject before persistence/DTO/effects. Source write guard covers canonical proposal and bounded actual edit candidate using existing public Read Service plus original SHA/owner CAS. Existing customer contents are not auto-deleted/modified; contaminated read is denied to SDK and contaminated old history remains unchanged with no usable partial restoration.\nEach run buffers text privately until safe terminal. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Explicit stop/switch suppresses new terminal monitor and cancels/drains existing monitors, eliminating observed organization_busy after stop while preserving async success/failure recommendations and ready-set parallelism.\nOrganization/Project/Memory Service/Provider private knownSecrets data guard docs/read/CAS/returned facts; no IO callback/grant expansion. No edits to Kernel/Adapter or package inputs. Current feature/api/design/change/changelog/navigation documents updated. Actual changedPaths19; six assigned source/test files unchanged.\nFinal candidate ff3efe208a387f3c73cf393ea8fe5f50a69728af47cd62cad4c60cfe6ea01470, frozen after all final edits. Same candidate all three configured checks exit0,candidateUnchanged true,0 fail/cancel/skip/todo:\nowner195/195, evidence .ans/runtime/legion-cli-role-owned-v2-key-guard-integration/evidence/desktop-known-key-guard-1-owner-ac03fbb5.log SHA2568d0b26bf1f3a281bcb87d47aba685347283cab6a1b236f7ba7b6691ec695e3f1;\nhost145/145, evidence .ans/runtime/legion-cli-role-owned-v2-key-guard-integration/evidence/desktop-known-key-guard-1-host-c2e1d2f3.log SHA256f40420446e98d340bcdd5aa6005bafa15e3d3ace073821c04432fbf899853cc5;\nparallel115/115, evidence .ans/runtime/legion-cli-role-owned-v2-key-guard-integration/evidence/desktop-known-key-guard-1-parallel-27fc3f95.log SHA25621799b142ca519df70e8e17d110fa550ac644faa0bb05df533e632dc52a7e7a3.\nReal random canaries exercised public Node first state/contaminated restore, 45000-char safe Human, actual Human question-answer, split SDK delta no prefix persisted/clean next turn, interaction/proposal/member/Supervisor, Source write/reconstructed edit, fact/Acceptance, automatic summary old memory unchanged, customer read original bytes, native Check and Shell stdout/stderr reconstruction with private knownKeys. Pure actual terminal merge regression covers non-prefix halves. Old three protocols, owner/claim-transfer/migration, strict nonce/profile revision, full Human, real repair/Acceptance, native install/build/delete/recovery, independent inference/same-role serial, all-instance cancel/zero late source writes/lease reacquisition still pass.\nAll historical failed/passing evidence retained. First failed setup hung its own fixture server; only proven child91480 was terminated with PID/PPID/start/command identity checked, task_ops recorded exit1. No skips, retries of rejected product operations or bare fallback; no verification/dispatch.\nLimits honestly retained: exact captured keys only, no arbitrary unknown/encoded-secret or full DLP guarantee. Reviewed external input TOCTOU and child reconstruction/direct writes to allowed tmp/outputs remain outside these gates; K private key count/byte budget failure closes rather than truncating keys. Initial intentionally contaminated customer/history fixtures remain preserved, not silently erased. Final isolated installed CLI/package and independent integration/review remain subsequent owner gates.","taskId":"legion-cli-role-owned-v2-key-guard-integration"}

Desktop encrypted metadata browsing stays read-only, active connection keys register before Human recording or role execution. Human valid commands including message/answer/profile/root/mapping/context/confirmation reject known values before invalidation, writes or grants; no silent redaction. Tool/control requests, PM/member text, role proposal/review, Acceptance/repair, roleMemories/summary, facts and Supervisor reject before persistence/DTO/effects. Source write guard covers canonical proposal and bounded actual edit candidate using existing public Read Service plus original SHA/owner CAS. Existing customer contents are not auto-deleted/modified; contaminated read is denied to SDK and contaminated old history remains unchanged with no usable partial restoration.\nEach run buffers text privately until safe terminal. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Explicit stop/switch suppresses new terminal monitor and cancels/drains existing monitors, eliminating observed organization_busy after stop while preserving async success/failure recommendations and ready-set parallelism.\nOrganization/Project/Memory Service/Provider private knownSecrets data guard docs/read/CAS/returned facts; no IO callback/grant expansion. No edits to Kernel/Adapter or package inputs. Current feature/api/design/change/changelog/navigation documents updated. Actual changedPaths19; six assigned source/test files unchanged.\nFinal candidate ff3efe208a387f3c73cf393ea8fe5f50a69728af47cd62cad4c60cfe6ea01470, frozen after all final edits. Pure actual terminal merge regression covers non-prefix halves. Old three protocols, owner/claim-transfer/migration, strict nonce/profile revision, full Human, real repair/Acceptance, native install/build/delete/recovery, independent inference/same-role serial, all-instance cancel/zero late source writes/lease reacquisition still pass.\nAll historical failed/passing evidence retained. First failed setup hung its own fixture server; only proven child91480 was terminated with PID/PPID/start/command identity checked, task_ops recorded exit1. No skips, retries of rejected product operations or bare fallback; no verification/dispatch.\nLimits honestly retained: exact captured keys only, no arbitrary unknown/encoded-secret or full DLP guarantee. Reviewed external input TOCTOU and child reconstruction/direct writes to allowed tmp/outputs remain outside these gates; K private key count/byte budget failure closes rather than truncating keys. Initial intentionally contaminated customer/history fixtures remain preserved, not silently erased. exact known-process-key guards in the unique Host, with runtime0.9.2 private Check/Shell knownKeys factory wiring. Node EnvironmentProfiles public resolve runs before first getState/history restore; Pipeline never reads process.env. Desktop encrypted metadata browsing stays read-only, active connection keys register before Human recording or role execution. Human valid commands including message/answer/profile/root/mapping/context/confirmation reject known values before invalidation, writes or grants; no silent redaction. Tool/control requests, PM/member text, role proposal/review, Acceptance/repair, roleMemories/summary, facts and Supervisor reject before persistence/DTO/effects. Source write guard covers canonical proposal and bounded actual edit candidate using existing public Read Service plus original SHA/owner CAS. Existing customer contents are not auto-deleted/modified; contaminated read is denied to SDK and contaminated old history remains unchanged with no usable partial restoration.\nEach run buffers text privately until safe terminal. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Explicit stop/switch suppresses new terminal monitor and cancels/drains existing monitors, eliminating observed organization_busy after stop while preserving async success/failure recommendations and ready-set parallelism.\nOrganization/Project/Memory Service/Provider private knownSecrets data guard docs/read/CAS/returned facts; no IO callback/grant expansion. No edits to Kernel/Adapter or package inputs. Current feature/api/design/change/changelog/navigation documents updated. Actual changedPaths19; six assigned source/test files unchanged.\nFinal candidate ff3efe208a387f3c73cf393ea8fe5f50a69728af47cd62cad4c60cfe6ea01470, frozen after all final edits. Pure actual terminal merge regression covers non-prefix halves. Old three protocols, owner/claim-transfer/migration, strict nonce/profile revision, full Human, real repair/Acceptance, native install/build/delete/recovery, independent inference/same-role serial, all-instance cancel/zero late source writes/lease reacquisition still pass.\nAll historical failed/passing evidence retained. exact known-process-key guards in the unique Host, with runtime0.9.2 private Check/Shell knownKeys factory wiring. Node EnvironmentProfiles public resolve runs before first getState/history restore; Pipeline never reads process.env. Desktop encrypted metadata browsing stays read-only, active connection keys register before Human recording or role execution. Human valid commands including message/answer/profile/root/mapping/context/confirmation reject known values before invalidation, writes or grants; no silent redaction. Tool/control requests, PM/member text, role proposal/review, Acceptance/repair, roleMemories/summary, facts and Supervisor reject before persistence/DTO/effects. Source write guard covers canonical proposal and bounded actual edit candidate using existing public Read Service plus original SHA/owner CAS. Existing customer contents are not auto-deleted/modified; contaminated read is denied to SDK and contaminated old history remains unchanged with no usable partial restoration.\nEach run buffers text privately until safe terminal. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Explicit stop/switch suppresses new terminal monitor and cancels/drains existing monitors, eliminating observed organization_busy after stop while preserving async success/failure recommendations and ready-set parallelism.\nOrganization/Project/Memory Service/Provider private knownSecrets data guard docs/read/CAS/returned facts; no IO callback/grant expansion. No edits to Kernel/Adapter or package inputs. Current feature/api/design/change/changelog/navigation documents updated. Actual changedPaths19; six assigned source/test files unchanged.\nFinal candidate ff3efe208a387f3c73cf393ea8fe5f50a69728af47cd62cad4c60cfe6ea01470, frozen after all final edits. Pure actual terminal merge regression covers non-prefix halves. Old three protocols, owner/claim-transfer/migration, strict nonce/profile revision, full Human, real repair/Acceptance, native install/build/delete/recovery, independent inference/same-role serial, all-instance cancel/zero late source writes/lease reacquisition still pass.\nAll historical failed/passing evidence retained. First failed setup hung its own fixture server; only proven child91480 was terminated with PID/PPID/start/command identity checked, task_ops recorded exit1. No skips, retries of rejected product operations or bare fallback; no verification/dispatch.\nLimits honestly retained: exact captured keys only, no arbitrary unknown/encoded-secret or full DLP guarantee. Reviewed external input TOCTOU and child reconstruction/direct writes to allowed tmp/outputs remain outside these gates; K private key count/byte budget failure closes rather than truncating keys. Initial intentionally contaminated customer/history fixtures remain preserved, not silently erased. Guard verifies actual timeline/member merge candidate, including non-prefix delta+terminal halves, before the first segment write; safe PM delta observations remain intact. Rejected PM context is closed/recreated from safe persistent facts; normal cancel captures original manager/promise and consumes actual terminal. Shell onOutput is serialized per request, preserving ordered archive/CAS and propagating failures; normalized archive text rechecked. Service经现有public
